<script>
/* ==========================================
*  グローバルナビ
* 2019/04/15 - 10:42
========================================== */
(function () {

  GNAV_SCREENTYPE = null;
  GNAV_STATE = "close";

  var $menubutton = $(".menuButton"); // トリガーにするボタン
  $gNavUnit = $("#g_navi"), // classを付け替える要素
    $gNavLink = $gNavUnit.find("a"), // グローバルナビリンク
    switchSize = 900, // SP / PC切り替えサイズ
    locationHref = location.href; // 現在のURL
  urlHash = location.hash, // 現在のURLハッシュ
    scrollTime = 1000, // スクロール時間
    scrollEasing = "swing", // スクロールイージング
    scrollOffsetYWide = -50; // PCサイズ時のスクロールオフセット値(px)
  scrollOffsetYNarrow = -100; // SPサイズ時スクロールオフセット値(px)

  // スムーススクロール
  function scrollToAnker(hash) {
    var target = $(hash),
      position = target.offset().top,
      offset = scrollOffsetYNarrow;
    if (GNAV_SCREENTYPE === "wide") {
      var offset = scrollOffsetYWide;
    }
    $("html,body").stop().animate({
      scrollTop: position + offset
    }, scrollTime, scrollEasing);
  }

  // Open グローバルナビ
  function openNav() {
    GNAV_STATE = "open";
    $gNavUnit.addClass("is-open");
  }

  // Close グローバルナビ
  function closeNav() {
    GNAV_STATE = "close";
    $gNavUnit.removeClass("is-open");
  }

  // URLハッシュ判定
  if (urlHash) {
    if ($(urlHash).length) {
      $(function () {
        $("html,body").stop().animate({
          scrollTop: 0
        }, 0);
      });
      $(window).on("load", function () {
        setTimeout(function () {
          scrollToAnker(urlHash);
        }, 10);
      });
    }
  }


  // スクリーンサイズチェック
  $(window).on("load resize", function () {
    var wW = $(window).innerWidth;
    if (wW > switchSize) {
      if (GNAV_SCREENTYPE === "wide") {
        return;
      }
      GNAV_SCREENTYPE = "wide";
      openNav();
    } else {
      if (GNAV_SCREENTYPE === "narrow") {
        return;
      }
      GNAV_SCREENTYPE = "narrow";
      closeNav();
    }
  });

  $menubutton.on("click", function () {
    if (GNAV_STATE === "close") {
      openNav();
    } else {
      closeNav();
    }
    return false;
  });

  $gNavLink.on("click", function () {
    var thisHash = this.hash,
      $target = $(thisHash),
      thisFilename = locationHref.replace(/#.*$/, ""),
      targetHref = $(this).get(0).href.replace(/#.*$/, "");
    if ($(this).attr("target")) {
      return;
    }
    if (!$target.length) {
      return;
    };
    if (targetHref.indexOf(thisFilename) < 0) {
      return;
    }
    if (GNAV_SCREENTYPE === "narrow") {
      closeNav();
    }
    scrollToAnker(thisHash);
    window.history.pushState(null, null, thisHash);
    return false;
  });

})();

$(function () {
  $(window).on("load resize", function () {
    let gNavHeight = $("#g_navi").outerHeight();
    $("#container").css("padding-top",gNavHeight);
  });
});
</script>	</div>

<script>
const monthnames = {
  1:['1月','January'],
  2:['2月','February'],
  3:['3月','March'],
  4:['4月','April'],
  5:['5月','May'],
  6:['6月','June'],
  7:['7月','July'],
  8:['8月','August'],
  9:['9月','September'],
  10:['10月','October'],
  11:['11月','November'],
  12:['12月','December'],
}
let setting = function() {}
function hankaku2Zenkaku(str) {
  return str.replace(/[Ａ-Ｚａ-ｚ０-９]/g, function(s) {
    return String.fromCharCode(s.charCodeAt(0) - 0xFEE0);
  });
}

function getArticleSetting(){
  const articleList = document.getElementById('list_06');
  const articleBoxs = articleList.getElementsByClassName('nwu_box');
  const prd = [];
  for(let i=0;i<articleBoxs.length;i++){
    const articleSetting = articleBoxs[i].getElementsByClassName('prdsetting');
    if(articleSetting.length){
      prd[i] = {};
      prd[i]['num'] = i;
      prd[i]['type'] = hankaku2Zenkaku(articleSetting[0].getAttribute('data-type'));
      const salesStart = hankaku2Zenkaku(articleSetting[0].getAttribute('data-order-start'));
      const salesStarts = salesStart.split('/');
      prd[i]['salesStartYear'] = salesStarts[0];
      prd[i]['salesStartMonth'] = salesStarts[1]
      prd[i]['salesStartDay'] = salesStarts[2]
      prd[i]['orderStart'] = hankaku2Zenkaku(articleSetting[0].getAttribute('data-order-start'));
      prd[i]['orderEnd'] = hankaku2Zenkaku(articleSetting[0].getAttribute('data-order-end'));
      prd[i]['link'] = articleBoxs[i].getElementsByTagName("a")[0].href;
      prd[i]['img'] = articleBoxs[i].getElementsByTagName("img")[0];
      const articleTitle = articleBoxs[i].getElementsByClassName("title")[0];
      prd[i]['title'] = articleTitle.getElementsByTagName("a")[0].textContent;
      prd[i]['original'] = articleSetting[0].getAttribute('original_title');
      prd[i]['resale'] = articleSetting[0].getAttribute('resale');
    }else{
      console.log(articleBoxs[i].getElementsByTagName("a")[0].href+'の公開情報が設定されていません');
    }
  }
  prd.sort((a, b) => b.salesStartDay - a.salesStartDay);
  prd.sort((a, b) => b.salesStartMonth - a.salesStartMonth);
  prd.sort((a, b) => b.salesStartYear - a.salesStartYear);
  setting.prototype.prdAll = prd;
}
function setProduct(){
  const cms_block = document.getElementById('cms_block');
  cms_block.innerHTML = '';
  const select = _getFilter();
  const prds = _prdFilter();
  const addHash = _serchHash();
  location.hash = addHash;
  if(select.year == 0){
    _printPrdAll()
  }else{
    _printPrdLayout(select.year)
  }
  function _printPrdBox(prdData){
    const productBox = document.createElement('li');
    productBox.classList.add('productBox');

    const productBoxLink = document.createElement('a');
    productBoxLink.href=prdData.link+addHash;

    const productBoxImg = document.createElement('div');
    productBoxImg.classList.add('productBox__img');
    const productBoxImgImg = prdData.img;
    productBoxImg.appendChild(productBoxImgImg);
    productBoxLink.appendChild(productBoxImg);

    const productBoxBody = document.createElement('div');
    productBoxBody.classList.add('productBox__body');
    const nowDate = new Date();
    if (prdData.orderStart != undefined && prdData.orderStart != "") {
        prdData.orderStart = new Date(prdData.orderStart);
    } else {
        prdData.orderStart = nowDate;
    }
    if (prdData.orderEnd != undefined && prdData.orderEnd != "") {
      prdData.orderEnd = new Date(prdData.orderEnd);
    }else{
      prdData.orderEnd = -1
    }

    if (prdData.orderStart <= nowDate && (prdData.orderEnd === -1 || nowDate <= prdData.orderEnd)) {
      const productBoxLabel = document.createElement('span');
      productBoxLabel.classList.add('productBox__label')
      productBoxLabel.append('受注中');
      productBoxImg.appendChild(productBoxLabel)
    }
    if(prdData.resale != 0){
      const productBoxLabel = document.createElement('span');
      productBoxLabel.classList.add('productBox__label')
      productBoxLabel.classList.add('-resale')
      productBoxLabel.append('再販');
      productBoxImg.appendChild(productBoxLabel)
    }
    const productBoxH3 = document.createElement('h3');
    productBoxH3.append(prdData.title);
    productBoxBody.appendChild(productBoxH3)
    const productBoxP = document.createElement('p');
    productBoxP.append(prdData.original);
    productBoxBody.appendChild(productBoxP)
    

    productBoxLink.appendChild(productBoxBody);
    productBox.appendChild(productBoxLink);

    return productBox;
  }
  function _serchHash(){
    let result = "";
    if(select.type != 0 || select.year != 0 || select.month != 0){
      result = '#';
      if(select.type != 0){
        result += 't'+select.type;
      }
      if(select.year != 0){
        if(result != '#'){ result += ','; }
        result += 'y'+select.year;
      }
      if(select.month != 0){
        if(result != '#'){ result += ','; }
        result += 'm'+select.month;
      }
    }
    return result;
  }
  function _printPrdList(monthData){
    const productList = document.createElement('ul');
    productList.classList.add('productList');
    for(let i=0;i<monthData.length;i++){
      productList.appendChild(_printPrdBox(monthData[i]));
    }

    return productList;
  }
  function _printPrdBlock(year,month){
    let monthData = prds.filter(prd => prd["salesStartYear"] == year && prd["salesStartMonth"] == month);
    if(select.type != 0){
      monthData = monthData.filter(monthData => monthData["type"] == select.type);
    }
    let productBlock = "";
    if(monthData.length || select.month != 0){
      productBlock = document.createElement('div');
      productBlock.classList.add('productBlock');
      const productBlockHead = document.createElement('div');
      productBlockHead.classList.add('productBlock__head');
      const productBlockHeadJA = document.createElement('span');
      productBlockHeadJA.classList.add('productBlock__head-ja');
      productBlockHeadJA.append(monthnames[month][0]);
      const productBlockHeadEN = document.createElement('span');
      productBlockHeadEN.classList.add('productBlock__head-en');
      productBlockHeadEN.append(monthnames[month][1]);
      productBlockHead.append(productBlockHeadJA,productBlockHeadEN);
      productBlock.appendChild(productBlockHead)
      if(monthData.length){
          const productBlockBody = document.createElement('div');
          productBlockBody.classList.add('productBlock__body');

          productBlockBody.appendChild(_printPrdList(monthData));
          productBlock.appendChild(productBlockBody)
      }
    }else{
      productBlock = document.createTextNode("");
    }
    return productBlock;
  }
  function _printPrdAll(){
    let prdYears = [];
    const prds = _prdFilter();
    for(let i=0;i<prds.length;i++){
      if(prdYears.indexOf(prds[i].salesStartYear)==-1){
        prdYears.push(prds[i].salesStartYear);
      }
    }
    _prdYearBtnFilter(prdYears)
    for(let i=0;i<prdYears.length;i++){
      _printPrdLayout(prdYears[i])
    }
  }
  function _printPrdLayout(year){
    const product = document.createElement('div');
    product.classList.add('product','product_fadein');

    const productHead = document.createElement('div');
    productHead.classList.add('product__head');
    productHead.append(year)

    const productBody = document.createElement('div');
    productBody.classList.add('product__body');
    if(select.month == 0){
      for(let i=12;i>0;i--){
        productBody.appendChild(_printPrdBlock(year,i))
      }
    }else{
      productBody.appendChild(_printPrdBlock(year,select.month))
    }

    product.appendChild(productHead)
    product.appendChild(productBody)
    cms_block.appendChild(product)
  }
  function _prdYearBtnFilter(years){
    const choiceYears = document.getElementsByClassName('choiceYear');
    for(let i=0;i<choiceYears.length;i++){
      const choiceYear = choiceYears[i];
      if(choiceYear.getAttribute('data-year') == 0 || years.includes(choiceYear.getAttribute('data-year'))){
        choiceYear.classList.remove('-disable')
      }else{
        choiceYear.classList.add('-disable')
      }
    }
  }
  function _prdYearFilter(year) {
    const result = setting.prototype.prdAll.filter(function (p) {
      return (select.type == 0 || p.type == select.type) && (year == 0 || p.salesStartYear == year);
    });
    return result;
  }
  function _prdFilter() {
    const result = setting.prototype.prdAll.filter(function (p) {
      return (select.type == 0 || p.type == select.type) && (select.year == 0 || p.salesStartYear == select.year) && (select.month == 0 || p.salesStartMonth == select.month);
    });
    return result;
  }
  function _getFilter(){
    const result = [];
    const crtType = document.getElementsByClassName('choiceType is-crt')[0];
    const crtYear = document.getElementsByClassName('choiceYear is-crt')[0];
    const crtMonth = document.getElementsByClassName('choiceMonth is-crt')[0];
    if(crtType){
      result.type = crtType.getAttribute('data-type');
    }else{
      result.type = 0;
    }
    if(crtYear){
      result.year = crtYear.getAttribute('data-year');
    }else{
      result.year = 0;
    }
    if(result.year != 0 && crtMonth){
      result.month = crtMonth.getAttribute('data-month');
    }else{
      result.month = 0;
    }
    return result;
  }
}
function setFilter(){
  const changeBtn = document.querySelectorAll('.choiceType, .choiceYear, .choiceMonth');
  _setBtn();
  function _setBtn(){
    for(let i=0;i<changeBtn.length;i++){
      changeBtn[i].addEventListener('click', function(e){
        _filterClick(e)
      }, false);
    }
    _firstFilter();
  }
  function _firstFilter(){
    const hash = location.hash;
    const hashTxt = hash.replace("#","")
    let target = [0,0,0];
    if(hashTxt.length){
      const hashs = hashTxt.split(",");
      for(let i=0;i<hashs.length;i++){
        switch(hashs[i].charAt(0)){
          case 't':
            target[0] = hashs[i].substring(1, hashs[i].length)
            break
          case 'y':
            target[1] = hashs[i].substring(1, hashs[i].length)
            break
          case 'm':
            target[2] = hashs[i].substring(1, hashs[i].length)
            break
        }
      }
    }
    _changeMonth(target[2],false)
    _changeYear(target[1],false)
    _changeType(target[0],false)
  }
  function _filterClick(e){
    const _this = e.target;
    if(_this.classList.contains("choiceMonth")){
      _changeMonth(_this.getAttribute('data-month'))
    }else{
      _changeMonth(0)
      if(_this.classList.contains("choiceYear")){
        _changeYear(_this.getAttribute('data-year'))
      }else{
        _changeYear(0)
        if(_this.classList.contains("choiceType")){
          _changeType(_this.getAttribute('data-type'))
          if(_this.getAttribute('data-year') != null){
            _changeYear(_this.getAttribute('data-year'))
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  function _yearLabel(){
    const yearLabel = document.getElementById('yearLabel');
    const crtYear = document.getElementsByClassName('choiceYear is-crt')[0];
    yearLabel.textContent = crtYear.textContent;
  }
  function _monthDisable(crt){
    const monthBtns = document.getElementsByClassName('choiceMonth');
    if(crt == 0){
      for(let i=0;i<monthBtns.length;i++){
        monthBtns[i].classList.add('disable')
      }
    }else{
      for(let i=0;i<monthBtns.length;i++){
        monthBtns[i].classList.remove('disable')
      }
    }
  }
  function _changeType(crt,set=true){
    _buttonCrt('choiceType',crt,'data-type',set)
  }
  function _changeYear(crt,set=true){
    _buttonCrt('choiceYear',crt,'data-year',set);
    _monthDisable(crt);
    _yearLabel();

  }
  function _changeMonth(crt,set=true){
    _buttonCrt('choiceMonth',crt,'data-month',set)
  }
  
  function _buttonCrt(className,crt,attrName,set=true){
    const btns = document.getElementsByClassName(className);
    for(let i=0;i<btns.length;i++){
      if(btns[i].getAttribute(attrName) == crt){
        btns[i].classList.add('is-crt');
      }else{
        btns[i].classList.remove('is-crt');
      }
    }
    if(set){
      setProduct()
    }
  }
}
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(){
  if(document.getElementById('list_06') != null){
    getArticleSetting();
    setFilter();
    setProduct();
  }else{
    const prdTypes = {1:'AMAKUNI',2:'AMAKUNI機神',3:'AMAKUNI真打',4:'AMAKUNI TECH'};
    const prdsetting = document.getElementsByClassName('prdsetting');
    if(prdsetting.length){
      const prdsettingType = hankaku2Zenkaku(prdsetting[0].getAttribute('data-type'));
      const panLi = document.createElement('li');
      panLi.append(prdTypes[prdsettingType]);
      
      document.getElementById('pan').children[0].appendChild(panLi)
    }
  }
});

$(function() {
  $(".termSelect__year").click(
  function() {
    $(this).toggleClass("open");
  });
  $("#cms_block").addClass("show");
});

// Title書換
 function changeTitle() {
    const productName = $('.product--name').text();
    const pageTitle = productName;
    document.title = productName + ' - AMAKUNI';
    $('#pan ul li:last').text(productName);
  }
$(function () {
  changeTitle();
});
</script>

</div>
</div>

/* ==========================================
*
*  Swiper.js
*
========================================== */

$(function(){
  $.when(
    $(".tp5 > li").attr("class", "swiper-slide"),
    $(".tp5").wrapInner('<div class="swiper-wrapper">'),
    $(".tp5 .swiper-wrapper").after('  <div class="swiper-button-prev"></div><div class="swiper-button-next"></div>'),
    $(".tp5").after('<div class="swiper-thumb_wrapper"><div class="swiper-thumb"></div><div class="swiper-button-prev thumb"></div><div class="swiper-button-next thumb"></div></div>'),
    $(".tp5 .swiper-wrapper").clone().appendTo(".swiper-thumb")
  ).done(function(){
    var sliderSelector = '.swiper-thumb'; // サムネコンテナ
    var slidesPerView = 8;
    var $slide = $(sliderSelector + ' .swiper-slide');
    var slidable = $slide.length > slidesPerView;
    var containerModifierClass =  slidable ? 'is-active-' : 'swiper-';
    var swiper = new Swiper(".swiper-thumb", {
      containerModifierClass: containerModifierClass,
      loopAdditionalSlides: false,
      spaceBetween: 5,
      slidesPerView: 5.5,
      freeMode: false,
      watchSlidesProgress: true,
      navigation: {
        nextEl: '.swiper-button-next.thumb',
        prevEl: '.swiper-button-prev.thumb',
      },
      breakpoints: {
        601: {
          slidesPerView: slidesPerView,
      },
    }
    });
    var swiper2 = new Swiper(".tp5", {
      loop: true,
      spaceBetween: 0,
      autoHeight: true,
//      initialSlide: 1,
      thumbs: {
        swiper: swiper,
      },
      navigation: {
        nextEl: '.swiper-button-next',
        prevEl: '.swiper-button-prev',
      },
      zoom: {
        maxRatio: 3,
      },
    });
  });

});

  $(".nwu_box .order_timer").each(function(index, element) {

    return true;
  });
});

/*子要素取得*/
$(function() {
  const prdsetting = $('.prdsetting');
  $(".block").has(".block_forming").each(function(){
    $(this).addClass("productBlock_forming");
  });
  $(".block").has("ul.product--product_detail_wrap").each(function(){
    $(this).find("ul.product--product_detail_wrap").after(orderPeriod(prdsetting));
  });
  $(".block").has(".smoothSkin").each(function(){
    $(this).addClass("smoothSkin").find("ul.product--product_detail_wrap").after('<div class="specialProcess"></div>');
  });
  $(".block").has(".amiami_collab").each(function(){
    $(".attention").before('<div class="collaboMark"></div>');
  });
  $(".block").has(".product--catch").each(function(){
    $(this).addClass("productBlock--catch");
  });
  $(".block").has(".product--intro").each(function(){
    $(this).addClass("productBlock--intro");
  });
  $(".block").has(".product--detail").each(function(){
    $(this).addClass("productBlock--detail");
  }); 
  $(".block").has(".product--attent").each(function(){
    $(this).addClass("productBlock--attent");
  }); 
  $(".block").has(".product--store").each(function(){
    $(this).addClass("productBlock--store");
  });
  $(".productBlock--store .read a").text("ご予約はこちら");
});
function dateKanji(date){
  const dates = date.split('/');
  const result = dates [0]+"年"+dates [1]+"月"+dates [2]+"日";
  const undefined = result;
  console.log(dates);
  return result;
}
function orderPeriod(setting){
  const result = $('<div>').addClass('orderPeriod');
  const prdsettingOrderStart = dateKanji(setting.data('order-start'));
  const prdsettingOrderEnd = dateKanji(setting.data('order-end'));
  console.log(prdsettingOrderStart);
  if(prdsettingOrderEnd.indexOf('undefined') >= 0){
    $('#cms_block').addClass('orderEndNull');
  } else if(prdsettingOrderStart.indexOf('undefined') >= 0){
    $('#cms_block').addClass('orderPeriodNull');
  }
  const inner = $('<p>').append('<b>受注期間</b>'+'<u>'+prdsettingOrderStart+'～</u><u class="orderEnd">'+prdsettingOrderEnd+'</u>');
  result.append(inner)
  return result;
}
